+test("D109 named exceptions: exact files and the public-key suffix are admitted; their siblings and symlink tricks stay refused; expiry ends them", (t) => {
+  if (process.platform === "win32") { t.skip("POSIX host paths"); return; }
+  const table = policyModule.PASSIVE_READ_CREDENTIAL_ROOTS.namedExceptions;
+  assert.ok(Array.isArray(table) && Object.isFrozen(table) && table.length >= 5, "the exceptions are a frozen table in the declaration");
+  for (const entry of table) {
+    for (const field of ["id", "path", "kind", "reason", "owner", "decision", "expires"]) assert.equal(typeof entry[field], "string", `${entry.id} lacks ${field}`);
+    assert.match(entry.expires, /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/u);
+    assert.ok(Object.isFrozen(entry));
+  }
+  const { admits } = d109();
+  for (const path of ["/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts", "~/.ssh/known_hosts", "/home/alice/.ssh/config", "/home/alice/.ssh/id_ed25519.pub",
+    "/home/alice/.aws/config", "/home/bob/.ssh/known_hosts", "/home/bob/.ssh/id_rsa.pub"]) {
+    assert.equal(admits(path), true, `${path} is a named exception`);
+  }
+  for (const path of ["/home/alice/.ssh/id_ed25519", "/home/alice/.ssh/authorized_keys", "/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts.old", "/home/alice/.ssh/sub/known_hosts",
+    "/home/alice/.aws/credentials", "/home/alice/.aws/config.d/x", "/home/alice/.ssh/.pub", "/home/alice/.ssh/nested/key.pub"]) {
+    assert.equal(admits(path), false, `${path} must stay refused`);
+  }
+  const trick = d109("/home/alice", { links: { "/home/alice/.ssh/alias.pub": "/home/alice/.ssh/id_rsa", "/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts": "/home/alice/.ssh/id_rsa" } });
+  assert.equal(trick.admits("/home/alice/.ssh/alias.pub"), false, "a .pub name that resolves to a private key");
+  assert.equal(trick.admits("/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts"), false, "a known_hosts link to a private key");
+  const expired = Date.parse("2099-01-01T00:00:00Z");
+  assert.equal(admits("/home/alice/.ssh/known_hosts", { now: expired }), false, "an expired exception is ignored");
+});

+// SPDX-License-Identifier: SUL-1.0
+
+/**
+ * Author-identity and first-answers-receipt readers of the V3 onboarding library,
+ * extracted out of project-onboarding-v3.mjs (which sits on its architecture line
+ * bound) so the pending-ask computation has room to change. Behaviour of every
+ * extracted function is unchanged except unresolvedAuthorIdentityKeys(), which now also
+ * treats a Git author held by the typed intake record as answered (D98). This module imports nothing from
+ * project-onboarding-v3.mjs (no import cycle).
+ */
+import { isSuccessfulSpawn } from "./successful-spawn.mjs";
+import { readOnboardingIntakeCheckpoint } from "./onboarding-continuity.mjs";
+import { resolveInitialAnswersState } from "./onboarding-initial-answers-state.mjs";
+
+export const PROJECT_ONBOARDING_INITIAL_ANSWERS_RECEIPT_SCHEMA = "pipeline.onboarding-initial-answers.v1";
+// A generous single-line bound for a real name or email address -- the same
+// role KICKOFF_GOAL_MAX_BYTES plays for the kickoff goal, kept local here
+// rather than imported so this stays independent of onboarding-continuity.mjs.
+export const AUTHOR_IDENTITY_FIELD_MAX_BYTES = 320;
+
+function validAuthorValue(value) {
+  return typeof value === "string" && value.trim().length > 0 && value.length <= AUTHOR_IDENTITY_FIELD_MAX_BYTES && !/[\r\n\0]/u.test(value);
+}
+
+/**
+ * The Git author a read intake checkpoint holds, as `{ name, email }`, or null when the record is
+ * absent, not present, or does not hold BOTH a valid name and a valid email. Never invents a value.
+ */
+export function intakeGitAuthor(checkpoint) {
+  const author = checkpoint?.status === "present" ? checkpoint.value?.values?.gitAuthor : null;
+  return validAuthorValue(author?.name) && validAuthorValue(author?.email)
+    ? { name: author.name, email: author.email } : null;
+}
+
+function intakeRecordHoldsGitAuthor(root, fs) {
+  try {
+    return intakeGitAuthor((fs.readOnboardingIntakeCheckpoint ?? readOnboardingIntakeCheckpoint)({
+      rootDir: root, repositoryCapability: "local", spawn: fs.spawnSync,
+    })) !== null;
+  } catch {
+    return false;
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Ask — never invent — the repository's commit author when it is unresolved.
+ *
+ * BOTH 2026-08-09 greenfield runs lost a PO turn to `Author identity unknown` at
+ * their first commit: onboarding initialized the repository and never looked at
+ * whether anything could commit into it. The agent then hit it several steps
+ * later, mid-implementation, where only the human could answer. A warn-only
+ * diagnostic (2026-08-09) fixed the discovery timing but not the interruption:
+ * it sat as one passive entry in the generic `diagnostics` array, next to dozens
+ * of unrelated diagnostic kinds, easy to miss and never blocking or asking
+ * anything (backlog: 2026-08-10-git-identity-warn-only-diagnostic-does-not-meet-
+ * po-expectation.md). This asks instead, at the exact same point, using the same
+ * `collect-input` shape `collectGoalAction()` already uses for the kickoff goal.
+ *
+ * This deliberately still does NOT set `user.name`/`user.email` itself. An
+ * author identity is a claim about who a human is; a seed inventing one would
+ * put a fabricated name in permanent history, which is worse than the stop it
+ * prevents — that safety property carries over unchanged; only the delivery
+ * mechanism (ask, not warn) changed.
+ *
+ * 2026-08-17 refinement (backlog: 2026-08-17-git-identity-must-be-set-
+ * immediately-before-first-commit-not-at-setup-time.md): a live Codex
+ * happy-path restart test showed the calling agent asking early as intended,
+ * then also running `git config` immediately, before onboarding readiness --
+ * the guidance below now explicitly defers the `git config` write until
+ * immediately before the repository's first commit; the ask itself is
+ * unchanged.
+ *
+ * Non-fatal by construction: an unreadable Git, a host-managed mount this seed
+ * does not own, or any probe failure resolves to "nothing missing" rather than
+ * a false alarm.
+ */
+export function unresolvedAuthorIdentityKeys(root, hostManaged, fs) {
+  if (hostManaged) return [];
+  // A complete root-bound answer is durable consent to hold these values,
+  // not evidence that Git config has already been written. Use this same
+  // reader for the initial side-channel ask AND the later intake ask.
+  const receipt = initialAnswersReceipt(root, fs);
+  if (validAuthorValue(receipt?.gitAuthorName) && validAuthorValue(receipt?.gitAuthorEmail)) return [];
+  const configured = (key) => {
+    try {
+      // Only repository-local identity satisfies this check.  A global or
+      // system value is surfaced separately as a proposed candidate with
+      // provenance; it is never treated as repository consent.
+      const probe = fs.spawnSync("git", ["config", "--get", "--local", key], { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8" });
+      return isSuccessfulSpawn(probe) && String(probe.stdout ?? "").trim().length > 0;
+    } catch {
+      return true; // Unprobeable is not "missing" -- never ask on evidence we do not have.
+    }
+  };
+  const missing = ["user.name", "user.email"].filter((key) => !configured(key));
+  // D98 (ALF-K2-GITAUTHOR): a complete author the typed intake record already holds is an answered
+  // question, not a pending ask; scripts/onboarding-init.mjs takes the receipt's author from the record.
+  // A one-sided, absent or unreadable record holds nothing, so the caller keeps asking for both.
+  if (missing.length > 0 && intakeRecordHoldsGitAuthor(root, fs)) return [];
+  return missing;
+}
+
+// Read-only provenance for the identity a future commit would inherit. Git's
+// author environment overrides author.* config, which overrides user.* config.
+// A proposed value is never the PO's consent, even when it is repository-local.
+export function gitIdentityProvenance(root, fs) {
+  const result = {};
+  for (const key of ["user.name", "user.email"]) {
+    let selected = null;
+    let unreadable = false;
+    const environmentKey = key === "user.name" ? "GIT_AUTHOR_NAME" : "GIT_AUTHOR_EMAIL";
+    const environmentValue = fs.environment?.[environmentKey];
+    if (typeof environmentValue === "string" && environmentValue.length > 0) {
+      const value = environmentValue.trim();
+      selected = value.length > 0 && value.length <= AUTHOR_IDENTITY_FIELD_MAX_BYTES && !/[\r\n\0]/u.test(value)
+        ? { value, source: "environment", scope: environmentKey }
+        : { value: null, source: "unknown", scope: environmentKey };
+    }
+    for (const configKey of [key.replace(/^user\./u, "author."), key]) {
+      if (selected !== null) break;
+      for (const scope of ["local", "global", "system"]) {
+        try {
+          const probe = fs.spawnSync("git", ["config", `--${scope}`, "--get", configKey], { cwd: root, encoding: "utf8" });
+          if (probe?.status === 0 && String(probe.stdout ?? "").trim().length > 0) {
+            selected = { value: String(probe.stdout).trim(), source: "git-config", scope, configKey };
+            break;
+          }
+          if (probe?.error) unreadable = true;
+        } catch {
+          unreadable = true;
+        }
+      }
+    }
+    if (selected === null && key === "user.email" && typeof fs.environment?.EMAIL === "string"
+      && fs.environment.EMAIL.length > 0) {
+      const value = fs.environment.EMAIL.trim();
+      selected = value.length > 0 && value.length <= AUTHOR_IDENTITY_FIELD_MAX_BYTES && !/[\r\n\0]/u.test(value)
+        ? { value, source: "environment", scope: "EMAIL" }
+        : { value: null, source: "unknown", scope: "EMAIL" };
+    }
+    if (selected === null) {
+      selected = { value: null, source: unreadable ? "unknown" : "absent", scope: null };
+    }
+    result[key] = selected;
+  }
+  return result;
+}
+
+export function initialAnswersReceipt(root, fs, repositoryCapability = "local") {
+  try {
+    const path = resolveInitialAnswersState(root, repositoryCapability).receipt;
+    if (!fs.existsSync(path)) return null;
+    const stat = fs.lstatSync(path);
+    if (!stat.isFile() || stat.isSymbolicLink() || stat.nlink !== 1) return null;
+    const parsed = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(path, "utf8"));
+    if (parsed?.schema !== PROJECT_ONBOARDING_INITIAL_ANSWERS_RECEIPT_SCHEMA
+      || parsed.root !== root
+      || !["signature", "chat"].includes(parsed.pushApprovalPreference)
+      || (parsed.language !== undefined && parsed.language !== null && !["de", "en"].includes(parsed.language))) return null;
+    return parsed;
+  } catch {
+    return null;
+  }
+}

+// K12: a taker of the registry lock waits for a live holder, bounded. A hook runs under a 10 s host timeout
+// (hooks/hooks.json, the shortest relevant one) and still has its own work to do once it holds the lock, and
+// retryWhileSessionAuthorityLockHeld (native-goldfish-host-state.mjs) already loops around this call, so the
+// bound is short: a holder normally keeps the lock for milliseconds, 1.5 s covers a slow disk or a busy
+// machine and leaves the hook more than 8 s for its own work.
+export const SESSION_AUTHORITY_LOCK_WAIT_MS=1500;
+const SESSION_AUTHORITY_LOCK_POLL_MS=50;
+const lockSleep=ms=>Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)),0,0,ms);
+// K12: retired registry locks (`<stamp>-<hex>-registry-lock`, holding only the lock and its retire.json) are kept
+// for forensics, newest SESSION_AUTHORITY_RETIRED_LOCKS_KEPT only. Observed 2026-10-21: ~70 in 1.5 days, one per
+// killed hook, each identical in kind; the last 20 keep roughly the latest half-day of churn, which is what a
+// forensic read of "who died holding the lock" needs, and bound the directory. Directories of any other kind
+// (drift-heal, move-aside, take-over, source-update, ...) hold registry content or decisions and are never swept.
+export const SESSION_AUTHORITY_RETIRED_LOCKS_KEPT=20;
+function sweepRetiredLocks(repo){
+  try{
+    const base='agent-pipeline/session-authority-abandoned',dir=safePath(repo,base);
+    const names=readdirSync(dir).filter(name=>/^\d{14,}-[0-9a-f]{8}-registry-lock$/.test(name)).sort();
+    for(const name of names.slice(0,Math.max(0,names.length-SESSION_AUTHORITY_RETIRED_LOCKS_KEPT))){
+      const abs=safePath(repo,`${base}/${name}`),stat=lstatSync(abs);
+      if(stat.isSymbolicLink()||!stat.isDirectory())continue;
+      const inner=readdirSync(abs);
+      if(inner.some(file=>file!=='registry.lock'&&file!=='retire.json'))continue;
+      for(const file of inner)unlinkSync(safePath(repo,`${base}/${name}/${file}`));
+      rmdirSync(abs);
+    }
+  }catch{/* best effort: a sweep that fails leaves the forensic copies, never the lock */}
+}

+/**
+ * D112: a descriptor LISTED a moment ago may be retired by its owner before an inspector loads it. The listing
+ * already skips such a name; the inspectors' required load reports it as WT-SESSION-MISSING. That is a
+ * descriptor that is already removed, not an observation failure: it leaves the result. Every other failure
+ * (malformed, binding or digest mismatch) still throws, so a descriptor that is still present keeps its
+ * fail-closed verdict. The result arrays are index-aligned over the descriptors that remain.
+ */
+function inspectListedDescriptors(root, listed, { inspectRetirement, inspectExternal = null }) {
+  const skipIfRetired = (inspect) => {
+    try {
+      return inspect();
+    } catch (error) {
+      if (error?.code === "WT-SESSION-MISSING") return null;
+      throw error;
+    }
+  };
+  const options = (descriptor) => ({ expectedDescriptorSha256: descriptor.descriptorSha256 });
+  const retirements = listed.map((descriptor) => skipIfRetired(
+    () => inspectRetirement(root, descriptor.sessionId, options(descriptor)),
+  ));
+  const externals = listed.map((descriptor, index) => (retirements[index] === null || inspectExternal === null
+    ? undefined
+    : skipIfRetired(() => inspectExternal(root, descriptor.sessionId, options(descriptor)))));
+  const kept = listed.map((_, index) => index)
+    .filter((index) => retirements[index] !== null && externals[index] !== null);
+  return {
+    activeDescriptors: kept.map((index) => listed[index]),
+    orphanDescriptors: kept.map((index) => retirements[index]),
+    externalRetirements: kept.map((index) => externals[index]),
+  };
+}

+// SPDX-License-Identifier: SUL-1.0
+
+/**
+ * The admitted half of the ordinary Codex consult in the host bridge.
+ *
+ * The bridge calls this only after the host reported an explicit `admitted`
+ * export decision AND a sealed, host-owned executor was installed as a
+ * dependency (never as stdin input). The executor's result is not trusted: an
+ * answer is reported only when the returned receipt, execution record and
+ * answer digest bind the candidate, route, question, evidence and host result
+ * through `verifyAnsweredAdvisoryReceipt`. Any other outcome is a typed refusal
+ * with a repair. Because an attempt may already have started, a refusal here is
+ * deliberately NOT described as a no-child route selection.
+ *
+ * The bridge's own helpers (route resolution, receipt construction, atomic
+ * persistence, event emission) are handed in; this module has no I/O of its own.
+ */
+import { ANSWERED_RECEIPT_REFUSALS, verifyAnsweredAdvisoryReceipt } from "../lib/advisory-answered-receipt.mjs";
+import { createAdvisoryConsultationRecord } from "../lib/advisory-lifecycle-v2.mjs";
+import { AdvisoryReceiptAssuranceError } from "../lib/advisory-receipt-assurance.mjs";
+
+/**
+ * Every refusal of the ordinary Codex consult names what the user can do next.
+ * Nothing ran, or nothing is trusted, so the alternative is to continue without
+ * an Advisor (an on-demand Advisor is optional, ADR-0085). Runner-neutral and
+ * free of question/evidence content.
+ */
+export const ORDINARY_CONSULT_REPAIRS = Object.freeze({
+  "advisor-repository-export-declined": "Repository Advisor export consent is declined, so no data was exported and no model was called. Continue without an Advisor (an on-demand Advisor is optional), or set advisor_export.consent to approved in pipeline.user.yaml and ask again.",
+  "advisor-host-export-denied": "The host did not approve this export, so no data was exported and no model was called. Continue without an Advisor; do not retry through another command or provider.",
+  "ordinary-consult-host-route-unavailable": "Repository export consent is missing or the host route is unavailable, so no data was exported and no model was called. Continue without an Advisor, or record advisor_export.consent in pipeline.user.yaml and ask again.",
+  "ordinary-consult-host-callback-unavailable": "This host has no sealed executor installed for an ordinary consult, so no data was exported and no model was called. Continue without an Advisor; an on-demand Advisor is optional and no design stage requires one.",
+  "advisor-host-execution-unavailable": "The host-owned executor failed or returned no verifiable result, so no answer is reported and nothing the executor said is trusted. Continue without an Advisor, or ask again once the host executor is repaired; a consultation of this request is spent.",
+  "advisory_route_unavailable": "The registered advisory route could not be resolved for this candidate, so the executor was not called. Resolve the registered route and ask again, or continue without an Advisor.",
+});
+
+/** Returns the process exit code after emitting exactly one `advisory.completed` event. */
+export async function finishAdmittedOrdinaryConsult({ input, args, repositoryRoot, dependencies, consultationRecordPath, host }) {
+  const base = { schema: "pipeline.advisory-host.v1", type: "advisory.completed", answer: null, attempts: [], attemptTrailPath: null, routeSelectionPath: null, sandboxBinding: null, agentDecisionEvent: null };
+  const route = host.resolveRoute();
+  if (route === null) {
+    host.emit({ ...base, ok: false, code: "advisory_route_unavailable", repair: ORDINARY_CONSULT_REPAIRS.advisory_route_unavailable,
+      receiptPath: null, consultationRecord: null, consultationRecordPath: null, modelCalls: 0, launcherCalls: 0 });
+    return 2;
+  }
+  let outcome = null;
+  try { outcome = await dependencies.executeAdmittedConsult({ input: structuredClone(input), repoRoot: repositoryRoot }); } catch { outcome = null; }
+  const result = outcome?.advisoryResult;
+  let failure = null;
+  if (result?.ok === true && result.code === "answered" && typeof result.answer === "string") {
+    const bound = verifyAnsweredAdvisoryReceipt({ receipt: result.receipt, execution: outcome.execution, answer: result.answer,
+      demand: input.demand, route, dispatch: input.dispatch, evidenceSha256: input.demand.evidenceSha256, selection: outcome.sandboxBinding ?? null });
+    if (!bound.ok) failure = { code: bound.code, repair: bound.repair };
+  } else if (typeof result?.code === "string" && Object.hasOwn(ANSWERED_RECEIPT_REFUSALS, result.code)) {
+    failure = { code: result.code, repair: ANSWERED_RECEIPT_REFUSALS[result.code] };
+  } else {
+    failure = { code: "advisor-host-execution-unavailable", repair: ORDINARY_CONSULT_REPAIRS["advisor-host-execution-unavailable"] };
+  }
+  let persisted = null;
+  let receipt = failure === null ? result.receipt : null;
+  if (failure === null) {
+    try { persisted = host.writeJsonAtomic(args.receipt, receipt); } catch (error) {
+      if (!(error instanceof AdvisoryReceiptAssuranceError)) throw error;
+      failure = { code: `advisory_receipt_${error.status}`, repair: ORDINARY_CONSULT_REPAIRS["advisor-host-execution-unavailable"] };
+      receipt = null;
+    }
+  }
+  if (failure !== null) {
+    // Only the unavailable receipt is written; a no-child route selection would claim no attempt started.
+    receipt = host.receiptFor(input, route, { status: "unavailable", fallbackReason: "consult-unavailable" });
+    persisted = host.writeJsonAtomic(args.receipt, receipt);
+  }
+  const record = createAdvisoryConsultationRecord({ demand: input.demand, receipt, outcome: receipt.observed.status, completedAtMs: Date.now() });
+  if (!record.ok) throw new Error(record.code);
+  host.writeJsonAtomic(consultationRecordPath, record.record);
+  const common = { receiptPath: host.resolvePath(args.receipt), directoryDurability: persisted?.directoryDurability ?? null, consultationRecord: record.record, consultationRecordPath };
+  if (failure !== null) {
+    host.emit({ ...base, ...common, ok: false, code: failure.code, repair: failure.repair });
+    return 2;
+  }
+  const decision = await (dependencies.recordAdvisoryDecisionEvent ?? host.recordAdvisoryDecisionEvent)(receipt, { repoRoot: repositoryRoot });
+  host.emit({ ...base, ...common, ok: true, code: "answered", answer: result.answer,
+    attempts: [{ adapter: "host-consult", kind: "consult", runner: "codex", status: "answered" }],
+    sandboxBinding: structuredClone(outcome.sandboxBinding), agentDecisionEvent: decision });
+  return 0;
+}

+// SPDX-License-Identifier: SUL-1.0
+
+/**
+ * D98 (ALF-K2-GITAUTHOR): a Git author the typed intake record already holds is not asked again,
+ * and the first-answers receipt (which carries the author to the first commit) takes it from the
+ * record when no author flags are given. Kept in its own file because onboarding-init.test.mjs sits
+ * over its architecture line bound.
+ */
+import assert from "node:assert/strict";
+import { mkdtempSync, readFileSync, rmSync } from "node:fs";
+import { tmpdir } from "node:os";
+import { join } from "node:path";
+import test from "node:test";
+
+import { applyInitialOnboardingAnswers, driveOnboardingInit } from "./onboarding-init.mjs";
+import { applyOnboardingIntakeConsent } from "../lib/onboarding-continuity.mjs";
+import { unresolvedAuthorIdentityKeys } from "../lib/project-onboarding-v3-author-identity.mjs";
+
+const fresh = (label) => mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), `onboarding-init-intake-author-${label}-`));
+const dispose = (path) => rmSync(path, { recursive: true, force: true });
+const inputNames = (action) => (action?.inputs ?? []).map((input) => input?.name);
+const environment = (home) => {
+  const env = { ...process.env, PIPELINE_ONBOARDING_HOMEDIR_OVERRIDE: home, CODEX_THREAD_ID: "ambient-codex-fixture" };
+  for (const key of ["CLAUDECODE", "ANTIGRAVITY_AGENT", "AI_AGENT", "CODEX_SESSION_ID"]) delete env[key];
+  return env;
+};
+
+test("the initial answers fall back to the Git author the intake record holds, and an explicit author wins", () => {
+  const recorded = { name: "Intake PO", email: "intake@example.invalid" };
+  const receiptOf = (root) => JSON.parse(readFileSync(join(root, ".git", "agent-pipeline", "onboarding-initial-answers.json"), "utf8"));
+  for (const explicit of [null, { name: "Explicit PO", email: "explicit@example.invalid" }]) {
+    const root = fresh("root");
+    const home = fresh("home");
+    try {
+      const env = environment(home);
+      assert.equal(driveOnboardingInit({ rootDir: root, runner: "claude", env }).outcome, "pending-asks");
+      applyOnboardingIntakeConsent({ rootDir: root, granted: true, gitAuthor: recorded, language: "en", activate: true });
+      if (explicit === null) {
+        const asked = driveOnboardingInit({ rootDir: root, runner: "claude", env });
+        assert.equal(asked.outcome, "pending-asks", JSON.stringify(asked));
+        const names = inputNames(asked.pendingAsks[0]);
+        assert.equal(names.includes("gitAuthorName") || names.includes("gitAuthorEmail"), false, `the held author is not asked again: ${names.join(",")}`);
+      }
+      const applied = applyInitialOnboardingAnswers({
+        rootDir: root, runner: "claude", env, pushApproval: "chat", language: "en",
+        ...(explicit === null ? {} : { gitAuthorName: explicit.name, gitAuthorEmail: explicit.email }),
+      });
+      assert.equal(applied.ok, true, JSON.stringify(applied));
+      const held = receiptOf(root);
+      const expected = explicit ?? recorded;
+      assert.deepEqual([held.gitAuthorName, held.gitAuthorEmail], [expected.name, expected.email]);
+    } finally {
+      dispose(root);
+      dispose(home);
+    }
+  }
+});
+
+test("a one-sided or absent Git author in the intake record is still asked, and a one-sided explicit author is refused", () => {
+  const noFiles = { existsSync: () => false, spawnSync: () => ({ status: 1, stdout: "", stderr: "" }) };
+  const withRecord = (gitAuthor) => ({ ...noFiles, readOnboardingIntakeCheckpoint: () => ({ status: "present", value: { values: { gitAuthor } } }) });
+  const keys = (fs) => unresolvedAuthorIdentityKeys("/nonexistent-root", false, fs);
+  assert.deepEqual(keys(withRecord({ name: "Both", email: "both@example.invalid" })), []);
+  assert.deepEqual(keys(withRecord({ name: "Only Name", email: "" })), ["user.name", "user.email"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(keys(withRecord({ name: "", email: "only@example.invalid" })), ["user.name", "user.email"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(keys(withRecord(null)), ["user.name", "user.email"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(keys({ ...noFiles, readOnboardingIntakeCheckpoint: () => ({ status: "absent" }) }), ["user.name", "user.email"]);
+  assert.deepEqual(keys({ ...noFiles, readOnboardingIntakeCheckpoint: () => { throw new Error("unreadable"); } }), ["user.name", "user.email"]);
+  const root = fresh("root");
+  const home = fresh("home");
+  try {
+    const env = environment(home);
+    assert.equal(driveOnboardingInit({ rootDir: root, runner: "claude", env }).outcome, "pending-asks");
+    assert.deepEqual(applyInitialOnboardingAnswers({ rootDir: root, runner: "claude", env, pushApproval: "chat", language: "en", gitAuthorName: "Only Name" }),
+      { ok: false, code: "INITIAL-ANSWERS-GIT-IDENTITY-INCOMPLETE" });
+  } finally {
+    dispose(root);
+    dispose(home);
+  }
+});
